# PCA adversarial conformance matrix

Generated by `scripts/pca-conformance-all.mjs`. Cell: `A` = accept, `R:<check>` = reject with first failing check (reason class), `ERR` = driver raised instead of returning a verdict, `HANG`/`CRASH` = fault. Languages in this run: ts, go, python, ruby, php, dotnet, rust, java. Skipped: none.
